/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/debug/DebugLogger.kt | sed -n '1,145p' && nl -ba app/src/androidTest/java/com/envi/wispr/VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt | sed -n '1,52p' && nl -ba app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/paste/PasteAccessibilityService.kt | sed -n '22,60p' && nl -ba app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/paste/RecordingAccessibilityOverlay.kt | sed -n '20,34p'" in /Users/m4pro_sv/Developer/EnviousLabs/EnviousWispr-Android/.claude/worktrees/issue-194-content-free-diagnostics
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	package com.envi.wispr.debug
     2	
     3	import android.os.SystemClock
     4	import android.util.Log
     5	import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue
     6	import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * The one door onto logcat for EnviousWispr (#194): every diagnostic line in the app goes through here,
    10	 * and what goes through is structurally content-free.
    11	 *
    12	 * - A [Throwable] is rendered by [render] as its class name and ONE code location, never its message
    13	 *   and never its full stack trace: an exception message carries whatever the failing call was holding
    14	 *   (a transcript, a prompt, a URL with a key on it), and Android's own three-argument `Log.e`
    15	 *   prints the message and the whole trace. The cause chain contributes class names only.
    16	 * - There is no file sink. The shared-storage log this object once wrote (`/sdcard/EnviousWispr/debug.log`)
    17	 *   had no caller and would have been readable by any app with storage access.
    18	 * - Pipeline profiling: mark events, get a timing summary. Thread-safe (AtomicInteger, ConcurrentLinkedQueue).
    19	 *
    20	 * `DiagnosticsShapeTest` refuses any other `android.util.Log` caller in the app and any diagnostic line
    21	 * that carries exception text; `DebugLoggerRenderTest` proves the rendering.
    22	 */
    23	internal object DebugLogger {
    24	
    25	    private const val MAX_MARKERS = 500
    26	
    27	    /** Frames whose class starts with this are OUR code: the location where our code met the failure. */
    28	    private const val APP_PACKAGE_PREFIX = "com.envi.wispr."
    29	
    30	    /** Cause class names rendered before the chain is cut with `…`; a repeat cuts it earlier. */
    31	    internal const val MAX_CAUSE_CLASSES = 4
    32	
    33	    @Volatile
    34	    private var pipelineStartTime = 0L
    35	
    36	    private val markers = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<Pair<String, Long>>()
    37	    private val markerCount = AtomicInteger(0)
    38	
    39	    /**
    40	     * Start a new pipeline timing session. Clears all previous markers.
    41	     */
    42	    fun startPipeline() {
    43	        pipelineStartTime = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
    44	        markers.clear()
    45	        markerCount.set(0)
    46	        log("Pipeline", "START")
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    /**
    50	     * Record a named timing marker relative to pipeline start.
    51	     * Capped at [MAX_MARKERS] to prevent unbounded growth.
    52	     */
    53	    fun mark(tag: String, event: String) {
    54	        val elapsed = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - pipelineStartTime
    55	        if (markerCount.getAndIncrement() < MAX_MARKERS) {
    56	            markers.add(event to elapsed)
    57	        }
    58	        log(tag, "$event [+${elapsed}ms]")
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    /**
    62	     * Get a one-line summary of all pipeline markers and total time.
    63	     */
    64	    fun pipelineSummary(): String {
    65	        val total = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - pipelineStartTime
    66	        val sb = StringBuilder("Pipeline: ${total}ms total")
    67	        for ((event, time) in markers) {
    68	            sb.append(" | $event:${time}ms")
    69	        }
    70	        return sb.toString()
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    fun debug(tag: String, message: String) {
    74	        Log.d(tag, message)
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    fun log(tag: String, message: String) {
    78	        Log.i(tag, message)
    79	    }
    80	
    81	    fun warn(tag: String, message: String) {
    82	        Log.w(tag, message)
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    /**
    86	     * The throwable is rendered by [render]; it is never handed to `Log.e` itself, which would print its
    87	     * message and full trace.
    88	     */
    89	    fun error(tag: String, message: String, throwable: Throwable? = null) {
    90	        Log.e(tag, render(message, throwable))
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    /**
    94	     * `message` alone when there is no throwable; otherwise `"$message (Class at Frame.method:line)"`,
    95	     * followed by ` caused by X, Y` for the cause chain's class names.
    96	     *
    97	     * The frame is the first whose class is ours ([APP_PACKAGE_PREFIX]): for a failure thrown by our code
    98	     * that is the throw site; for one thrown by a library and caught by us it is the call our code made.
    99	     * With no such frame the top frame is used; with no frames at all (a throwable built with
   100	     * `writableStackTrace = false`, or a vendor override of `fillInStackTrace`) the location is `no frame`.
   101	     *
   102	     * The cause walk is iterative with identity tracking and stops at [MAX_CAUSE_CLASSES] names or at the
   103	     * first cause already seen, appending `…` when it was cut, so a cycle or a deep chain is bounded.
   104	     * The frames are read once, here, on an error path only.
   105	     */
   106	    internal fun render(message: String, throwable: Throwable?): String {
   107	        if (throwable == null) return message
   108	        val sb = StringBuilder(message)
   109	        sb.append(" (").append(className(throwable)).append(" at ").append(location(throwable)).append(')')
   110	        val seen = java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(java.util.IdentityHashMap<Throwable, Boolean>())
   111	        seen.add(throwable)
   112	        var cause = throwable.cause
   113	        var rendered = 0
   114	        var first = true
   115	        while (cause != null) {
   116	            if (!seen.add(cause) || rendered == MAX_CAUSE_CLASSES) {
   117	                sb.append(if (first) " caused by …" else ", …")
   118	                break
   119	            }
   120	            sb.append(if (first) " caused by " else ", ").append(className(cause))
   121	            first = false
   122	            rendered++
   123	            cause = cause.cause
   124	        }
   125	        return sb.toString()
   126	    }
   127	
   128	    private fun className(throwable: Throwable): String =
   129	        throwable.javaClass.simpleName.ifEmpty { throwable.javaClass.name.substringAfterLast('.') }
   130	
   131	    private fun location(throwable: Throwable): String {
   132	        val frames = throwable.stackTrace
   133	        if (frames.isEmpty()) return "no frame"
   134	        val frame = frames.firstOrNull { it.className.startsWith(APP_PACKAGE_PREFIX) } ?: frames[0]
   135	        return "${frame.className.substringAfterLast('.')}.${frame.methodName}:${frame.lineNumber}"
   136	    }
   137	}

Issue #194 code review — DONE

## Findings

1. **HYPOTHETICAL:** The four special-source guards can remain green while content leaks through a transformed value. `${text.take(20)}` bypasses the bare `$text` checks, `${host.lowercase()}` bypasses the host check, and `llama_log_callback { log_info(text); }` contains no direct `__android_log_print` while still reaching it. `app/src/test/java/com/envi/wispr/debug/DiagnosticsShapeTest.kt:89-113`

   Drop-in fix:
   - After removing the allowed `text.length` expression, assert no `\btext\b` remains in either diagnostic span.
   - After removing `ModelSourceHost.of(host).wire`, assert no `\bhost\b` remains on the source line.
   - Assert `callback.groupValues[1].isBlank()` instead of checking only for `__android_log_print`.
   - Add these three bypasses to a red control fixture.

2. **HYPOTHETICAL:** The general scanner has silent bypasses. `import android.util.Log as L` and `import android.util.*` evade `androidLogUses`; `this.log.warn(...)` and fully qualified `DebugLogger` calls are rejected because the token is preceded by `.`; and a `)` inside a string can terminate the naive span before a later `${e.message}`. The last case contradicts the KDoc claim that naive balancing can only fail loudly. `DiagnosticsShapeTest.kt:32-33,147-188`

   Drop-in fix:
   - Detect Log imports with `(?m)^\\s*import\\s+android\\.util\\.(?:Log|\\*)\\b`.
   - Permit `.` before recognized diagnostic tokens and recognize whitespace around receiver dots.
   - Replace the parenthesis loop with a small lexical scanner that ignores parentheses and commas inside escaped, regular and triple-quoted strings.
   - Add positive controls for an aliased Log import, `this.log.warn("x ${e.message}")`, and `DebugLogger.warn(TAG, "shape ) ${e.message}")`.
   - Change `span.contains(".message")` at line 195 to `Regex("""\\.message\\b""")` so `.messageCount` does not false-red. The bare `$it` false positive remains the stated name-based tradeoff; nonthrowable lambdas should bind a descriptive name.

3. **REPRODUCIBLE:** The plan promises positive controls for `Environment` and `FileWriter`, but neither appears in `POSITIVE` or `expected`; the owner test checks them without proving that its detector can go red. `DiagnosticsShapeTest.kt:77-84,118-142,227-258`

   Drop-in fix:
   - Add `fileSinkUses(path, text)` for `Environment` and `FileWriter`.
   - Include it in both fixture scans and the owner row.
   - Plant both tokens in `POSITIVE` and add both expected labels.

4. **REPRODUCIBLE:** Every JVM `Test` task reads `src/debug/java`, but only `src/androidTest/java` and the JNI bridge are declared as extra inputs. `testReleaseUnitTest` does not otherwise own debug sources, so a debug-only edit can replay a cached release green. `app/build.gradle.kts:103-109`

   Drop-in fix:
   ```kotlin
   tasks.withType<Test>().configureEach {
       inputs.dir("src/debug/java").withPathSensitivity(PathSensitivity.RELATIVE)
       inputs.dir("src/androidTest/java").withPathSensitivity(PathSensitivity.RELATIVE)
       inputs.file("../llama-android/src/main/cpp/s1_jni.cpp")
           .withPathSensitivity(PathSensitivity.RELATIVE)
   }
   ```

5. **REPRODUCIBLE:** `DebugLogger` overstates both the old sink’s exposure and the scanner’s scope. Modern scoped storage does not make an arbitrary shared path readable by every app with generic storage access, and the standalone, non-shipping `:accelerator-benchmark` module remains outside the scan with raw Log calls, including a throwable at `BenchmarkActivity.kt:114`. `app/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/debug/DebugLogger.kt:9-21`; `accelerator-benchmark/src/main/java/com/envi/wispr/benchmark/BenchmarkActivity.kt:8,62-114`

   Drop-in replacement:
   > The one door onto logcat for the shipped `:app` module. The deleted sink targeted shared external storage rather than app-private storage, had no caller, and lacked its required access. `DiagnosticsShapeTest` covers `:app` main, debug and androidTest sources plus the named JNI bridge; the standalone, non-shipping accelerator benchmark is outside this contract.

6. **REPRODUCIBLE:** New `DebugLogger` imports were appended after `java`, `kotlinx` or `org.junit` imports instead of joining the surrounding `com.envi.wispr` group. `SilenceStoppedTakeTranscribesDeviceTest.kt:20`; `VoicePipelineDeviceTest.kt:45`; `PolishServiceDeviceTest.kt:20`; `PasteAccessibilityService.kt:56`; `RecordingAccessibilityOverlay.kt:29`

   Drop-in fix: Move each `DebugLogger` import into the existing `com.envi.wispr...` block and sort that block consistently.

## Axes (A1..A7 found/not found)

- **A1 NOT FOUND:** The renderer itself adds no message content. Anonymous/local throwable classes use the binary-name fallback; frame class and method names are static code metadata. `IdentityHashMap` is valid on ART. The fifth cause and cycle behavior match the literal tests. `DebugLogger.kt:106-135`
- **A2 NOT FOUND:** All 29 replacements receive non-null `Throwable` values from `catch`, `Result.onFailure`, `getOrElse`, or `exceptionOrNull()?.let`. The loss of OS prose is intentional; typed ASR reasons remain separate.
- **A3 FOUND:** Levels and throwable migrations are correct, and visibility compiles across same-module debug/androidTest sources. Import placement needs the mechanical cleanup above.
- **A4 FOUND:** The current tree passes the naive scanner, but the constructed silent bypasses and `.messageCount` false positive require revision. Bare `$it` for a nonthrowable is a conservative, loud false positive under the declared name-based limit. `DiagnosticsShapeTest.kt:147-224`
- **A5 FOUND:** The KDoc needs to say `:app` and shared external storage, not every app or every module.
- **A6 FOUND:** The configuration is scoped to `:app`, so it does not affect benchmark tasks; the tracked JNI file is present. The missing `src/debug/java` input leaves `testReleaseUnitTest` cache-invalidating incompletely.
- **A7 FOUND:** D1-D4 behavior is implemented, but D5’s promised `Environment`/`FileWriter` control is missing. No other plan delta was found.

## VERDICT: FIX-THEN-RERUN
